                                                          each pope, culminating with the final pope, Peter the Roman, whose

                                                          Rome by Pope Innocent II. While there, he experienced a vision of
                                                          the future popes, including the last one, which he wrote down in a
                                                          series of cryptic phrases. According to the prophecy, the next pope is
